languages= ; \
if test "yes" = yes && test -d "../../po4a/man"; then \
	languages=`ls "../../po4a/man"`; \
fi; \
for lang in $languages; do \
	mans= ; \
	for man in xzdiff.1 xzgrep.1 xzmore.1 xzless.1; do \
		man="../../po4a/man/$lang/$man" ; \
		if test -f "$man"; then \
			mans="$mans $man"; \
		fi; \
	done; \
	make dist_man_MANS="$mans" man_MANS= \
			mandir="/usr/share/man/$lang" install-man; \
done; \
for lang in . $languages; do \
	for pair in xzdiff-xzcmp xzgrep-xzegrep xzgrep-xzfgrep xzdiff-lzdiff xzdiff-lzcmp xzgrep-lzgrep xzgrep-lzegrep xzgrep-lzfgrep xzmore-lzmore xzless-lzless; do \
		target=`echo $pair | sed 's/-.*$//' \
				| sed 's,x,x,'` && \
		link=`echo $pair | sed 's/^.*-//' \
				| sed 's,x,x,'` && \
		man1dir="/var/tmp/hokuto/xz-01/output/usr/share/man/$lang/man1" && \
		if test -f "$man1dir/$target.1"; then ( \
			cd "$man1dir" && \
			rm -f "$link.1" && \
			ln -s "$target.1" "$link.1" \
		); fi; \
	done; \
done

	-e 's,@prefix[@],/usr,g' \
	-e 's,@exec_prefix[@],/usr,g' \
	-e 's,@libdir[@],/usr/lib32,g' \
	-e 's,@includedir[@],/usr/include,g' \
	-e 's,@PACKAGE_URL[@],https://tukaani.org/xz/,g' \
	-e 's,@PACKAGE_VERSION[@],5.8.4,g' \

	-e 's,@LIBS[@],-lpthread ,g' \

		make dist_man_MANS="$man" man_MANS= \
			mandir="/usr/share/man/$lang" install-man; \
	fi; \
	man1dir="/var/tmp/hokuto/xz-01/build/DESTDIR/usr/share/man/$lang/man1" && \

		cd "$man1dir" && \

		done \
	); fi; \
done

make[4]: Entering directory '/var/tmp/hokuto/xz-01/build/src/scripts'
languages= ; \
if test "yes" = yes && test -d "../../po4a/man"; then \
	languages=`ls "../../po4a/man"`; \
fi; \
for lang in $languages; do \
	mans= ; \
	for man in xzdiff.1 xzgrep.1 xzmore.1 xzless.1; do \
		man="../../po4a/man/$lang/$man" ; \
		if test -f "$man"; then \
			mans="$mans $man"; \
		fi; \
	done; \
	make dist_man_MANS="$mans" man_MANS= \
			mandir="/usr/share/man/$lang" install-man; \
done; \
for lang in . $languages; do \
	for pair in xzdiff-xzcmp xzgrep-xzegrep xzgrep-xzfgrep xzdiff-lzdiff xzdiff-lzcmp xzgrep-lzgrep xzgrep-lzegrep xzgrep-lzfgrep xzmore-lzmore xzless-lzless; do \
		target=`echo $pair | sed 's/-.*$//' \
				| sed 's,x,x,'` && \
		link=`echo $pair | sed 's/^.*-//' \
				| sed 's,x,x,'` && \
		man1dir="/var/tmp/hokuto/xz-01/build/DESTDIR/usr/share/man/$lang/man1" && \
		if test -f "$man1dir/$target.1"; then ( \
			cd "$man1dir" && \
			rm -f "$link.1" && \
			ln -s "$target.1" "$link.1" \
		); fi; \
	done; \
done
